        function u19.Kill(p27, p28)

                BringEnemy()
                EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)

                if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool').ToolTip ~= 'Blox Fruit' then
                    _tp(p27.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0))
                else
                    _tp(p27.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0))
                end
                if RandomCFrame then
                    wait(0.5)
                    _tp(p27.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
                    wait(0.5)
                    _tp(p27.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 30, 0))

        function statsSetings(p39, p40)
            if p39 == 'Melee' then
                if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Melee', p40)
                end
            elseif p39 == 'Defense' then
                if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Defense', p40)
                end
            elseif p39 == 'Sword' then
                if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Sword', p40)
                end
            elseif p39 == 'Gun' then
                if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Gun', p40)
                end
            elseif p39 == 'Devil' and plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Demon Fruit', p40)
            end
        end
        function BringEnemy()
            if _B then
                local v41, v42, v43 = pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren())

                while true do
                    local v44

                    v43, v44 = v41(v42, v43)

                    if v43 == nil then
                        break
                    end
                    if v44:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (v44.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (v44.PrimaryPart.Position - PosMon).Magnitude <= 300) then
                        v44.PrimaryPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(PosMon)
                        v44.PrimaryPart.CanCollide = true
                        v44:FindFirstChild('Humanoid').WalkSpeed = 0
                        v44:FindFirstChild('Humanoid').JumpPower = 0

                        if v44.Humanoid:FindFirstChild('Animator') then
                            v44.Humanoid.Animator:Destroy()
                        end

                        plr.SimulationRadius = math.huge
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        function Useskills(p45, p46)
            if p45 == 'Melee' then
                weaponSc('Melee')

                if p46 == 'Z' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
                elseif p46 == 'X' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'X', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'X', false, game)
                elseif p46 == 'C' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'C', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'C', false, game)
                end
            elseif p45 == 'Sword' then
                weaponSc('Sword')

                if p46 == 'Z' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
                elseif p46 == 'X' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'X', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'X', false, game)
                end
            elseif p45 == 'Blox Fruit' then
                weaponSc('Blox Fruit')

                if p46 == 'Z' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
                elseif p46 == 'X' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'X', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'X', false, game)
                elseif p46 == 'C' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'C', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'C', false, game)
                elseif p46 == 'V' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'V', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'V', false, game)
                end
            elseif p45 == 'Gun' then
                weaponSc('Gun')

                if p46 == 'Z' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
                elseif p46 == 'X' then
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'X', false, game)
                    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'X', false, game)
                end
            end
            if p45 == 'nil' and p46 == 'Y' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Y', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Y', false, game)
            end
        end

        function GetConnectionEnemies(p51)
            local v52, v53, v54 = pairs(replicated:GetChildren())

            while true do
                local v55

                v54, v55 = v52(v53, v54)

                if v54 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v55:IsA('Model') and (typeof(p51) == 'table' and table.find(p51, v55.Name) or v55.Name == p51) and (v55:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and v55.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                    return v55
                end
            end

            local v56 = next
            local v57, v58 = game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()

            while true do
                local v59

                v58, v59 = v56(v57, v58)

                if v58 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v59:IsA('Model') and (typeof(p51) == 'table' and table.find(p51, v59.Name) or v59.Name == p51) and (v59:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and v59.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                    return v59
                end
            end
        end
        function LowCpu()
            local v60 = game
            local _Workspace = v60.Workspace
            local _Lighting = v60.Lighting
            local _Terrain = _Workspace.Terrain

            _Terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
            _Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
            _Terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
            _Terrain.WaterTransparency = 0
            _Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
            _Lighting.FogEnd = 9000000000
            _Lighting.Brightness = 0
            settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = 'Level01'

            local v64, v65, v66 = pairs(v60:GetDescendants())
            local v67 = true

            while true do
                local v68

                v66, v68 = v64(v65, v66)

                if v66 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v68:IsA('Part') or (v68:IsA('Union') or (v68:IsA('CornerWedgePart') or v68:IsA('TrussPart'))) then
                    v68.Material = 'Plastic'
                    v68.Reflectance = 0
                elseif v68:IsA('Decal') or v68:IsA('Texture') and v67 then
                    v68.Transparency = 1
                elseif v68:IsA('ParticleEmitter') or v68:IsA('Trail') then
                    v68.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
                elseif v68:IsA('Explosion') then
                    v68.BlastPressure = 1
                    v68.BlastRadius = 1
                elseif v68:IsA('Fire') or (v68:IsA('SpotLight') or (v68:IsA('Smoke') or v68:IsA('Sparkles'))) then
                    v68.Enabled = false
                elseif v68:IsA('MeshPart') then
                    v68.Material = 'Plastic'
                    v68.Reflectance = 0
                    v68.TextureID = 1.0385902758728955e16
                end
            end

            local v69, v70, v71 = pairs(_Lighting:GetChildren())

            while true do
                local v72

                v71, v72 = v69(v70, v71)

                if v71 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v72:IsA('BlurEffect') or (v72:IsA('SunRaysEffect') or (v72:IsA('ColorCorrectionEffect') or (v72:IsA('BloomEffect') or v72:IsA('DepthOfFieldEffect')))) then
                    v72.Enabled = false
                end
            end
        end

                return true
            end
        end
        function CheckBoat()
            local v73, v74, v75 = pairs(workspace.Boats:GetChildren())

            while true do
                local v76

                if v75 == nil then
                    break

                    return v76
                end
            end

            return false
        end

        function DropFruits()

            while true do

                if v117 == nil then
                    break

                if string.find(v118.Name, 'Fruit') then
                    EquipWeapon(v118.Name)
                    wait(0.1)

                    if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
                        plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
                    end

                    EquipWeapon(v118.Name)
                    plr.Character:FindFirstChild(v118.Name).EatRemote:InvokeServer('Drop')
                end
            end

            local v119, v120, v121 = pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren())

            while true do
                local v122

                v121, v122 = v119(v120, v121)

                if v121 == nil then
                    break

                    EquipWeapon(v122.Name)

        function GetIn(p124)

                if v127 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if type(v128) == 'table' and (v128.Name == p124 or (plr.Character:FindFirstChild(p124) or plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(p124))) then
                    return true
                end
            end

            return false
        end
        function GetM(p129)
            local v130, v131, v132 = pairs(replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory'))

            while true do
                local v133

                v132, v133 = v130(v131, v132)

                if v132 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if type(v133) == 'table' and (v133.Type == 'Material' and v133.Name == p129) then
                    return v133.Count
                end
            end

            return 0
        end

        function getInfinity_Ability(p139, p140)
            if Root then
                if p139 == 'Soru' and p140 then
                    local v141 = next
                    local v142, v143 = getgc()

                    while true do
                        local v144

                        v143, v144 = v141(v142, v143)

                        if v143 == nil then
                            break
                        end
                        if plr.Character.Soru and (typeof(v144) == 'function' and getfenv(v144).script == plr.Character.Soru) then
                            local v145 = next
                            local v146, v147 = getupvalues(v144)

                            while true do
                                local v148

                                v147, v148 = v145(v146, v147)

                                if v147 == nil then
                                    break
                                end
                                if typeof(v148) == 'table' then
                                    repeat
                                        wait(Sec)

                                        v148.LastUse = 0
                                    until not p140 or plr.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                elseif p139 == 'Energy' and p140 then
                    plr.Character.Energy.Changed:connect(function()
                        if p140 then
                            plr.Character.Energy.Value = Energy
                        end
                    end)
                elseif p139 == 'Observation' and p140 then
                    plr.VisionRadius.Value = math.huge
                end
            end
        end
        function Hop()
            pcall(function()
                for v149 = math.random(1, math.random(40, 75)), 100 do
                    local v150 = replicated.__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer(v149)
                    local v151 = next
                    local v152 = nil

                    while true do
                        local v153

                        v152, v153 = v151(v150, v152)

                        if v152 == nil then
                            break
                        end
                        if tonumber(v153.Count) < 12 then
                            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, v152)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        function _tp(p168)
            local _Character5 = plr.Character

            if _Character5 and _Character5:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                local _HumanoidRootPart = _Character5.HumanoidRootPart
                local _Magnitude = (p168.Position - _HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                local v172 = TweenInfo.new(_Magnitude / 300, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
                local u173 = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(_Part, v172, {CFrame = p168})

                if plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit == true then
                    _Part.CFrame = CFrame.new(_Part.Position.X, p168.Y, _Part.Position.Z)
                end

                u173:Play()
                task.spawn(function()
                    while u173.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing do
                        if not shouldTween then
                            u173:Cancel()

                            break
                        end

                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        function TeleportToTarget(p174)
            if (p174.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 1000 then
                _tp(p174)
            else
                _tp(p174)
            end
        end

        function BTP(p176)

                    _Position = _HumanoidRootPart2.Position

                end

                task.wait(0.5)

                if (p176.Position - _HumanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude <= 2000 then
                    return
                end
            end
        end

        function QuestBeta()
            QuestB()

            return {
                [0] = _G.FindBoss,
                [1] = bMon,
                [2] = Qdata,
                [3] = Qname,
                [4] = PosB,
            }
        end

                    PosQ = CFrame.new(-16541.0215, 57.3082275, 1051.46118, 0.0410757065, 0, -0.999156058, 0, 1, 0, 0.999156058, 0, 0.0410757065)
                    PosM = CFrame.new(-16602.1015625, 130.38734436035156, 1087.24560546875)
                elseif _Value == 2551 or _Value <= 2574 then
                    Mon = 'Serpent Hunter'
                    Qdata = 1
                    Qname = 'TikiQuest3'
                    NameMon = 'Serpent Hunter'
                    PosQ = CFrame.new(-16679.478515625, 176.74737548828125, 1474.3995361328125)
                    PosM = CFrame.new(-16679.478515625, 176.74737548828125, 1474.3995361328125)

        function MaterialMon()
            local _LocalPlayer3 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
            local _Character6 = _LocalPlayer3.Character

            if _Character6 then
                _Character6 = _LocalPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            end
            if _Character6 then
                function shouldRequestEntrance(p191, p192)
                    if p192 <= (_Character6.Position - p191).Magnitude then
                        replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', p191)
                    end
                end

                if World1 then
                    if SelectMaterial ~= 'Angel Wings' then
                        if SelectMaterial ~= 'Leather + Scrap Metal' then
                            if SelectMaterial ~= 'Magma Ore' then
                                if SelectMaterial == 'Fish Tail' then
                                    MMon = {
                                        'Fishman Warrior',
                                        'Fishman Commando',
                                        'Fishman Lord',
                                    }
                                    MPos = CFrame.new(61123, 19, 1569)
                                    SP = 'Default'

                                    local v193 = Vector3.new(61163.8515625, 5.342342376708984, 1819.7841796875)

                                    shouldRequestEntrance(v193, 17000)
                                end
                            else
                                MMon = {
                                    'Military Soldier',
                                    'Military Spy',
                                    'Magma Admiral',
                                }
                                MPos = CFrame.new(-5815, 84, 8820)
                                SP = 'Default'
                            end
                        else
                            MMon = {
                                'Brute',
                                'Pirate',
                            }
                            MPos = CFrame.new(-1145, 15, 4350)
                            SP = 'Default'
                        end
                    else
                        MMon = {
                            'Shanda',
                            'Royal Squad',
                            'Royal Soldier',
                            'Wysper',
                            'Thunder God',
                        }
                        MPos = CFrame.new(-4698, 845, -1912)
                        SP = 'Default'

                        local v194 = Vector3.new(-4607.82275, 872.54248, -1667.55688)

                        shouldRequestEntrance(v194, 10000)
                    end
                elseif World2 then
                    if SelectMaterial ~= 'Leather + Scrap Metal' then
                        if SelectMaterial ~= 'Magma Ore' then
                            if SelectMaterial ~= 'Ectoplasm' then
                                if SelectMaterial ~= 'Mystic Droplet' then
                                    if SelectMaterial ~= 'Radioactive Material' then
                                        if SelectMaterial == 'Vampire Fang' then
                                            MMon = {
                                                'Vampire',
                                            }
                                            MPos = CFrame.new(-6033, 7, -1317)
                                            SP = 'Default'
                                        end
                                    else
                                        MMon = {
                                            'Factory Staff',
                                        }
                                        MPos = CFrame.new(295, 73, -56)
                                        SP = 'Default'
                                    end
                                else
                                    MMon = {
                                        'Water Fighter',
                                    }
                                    MPos = CFrame.new(-3385, 239, -10542)
                                    SP = 'Default'
                                end
                            else
                                MMon = {
                                    'Ship Deckhand',
                                    'Ship Engineer',
                                    'Ship Steward',
                                    'Ship Officer',
                                }
                                MPos = CFrame.new(911.35827636719, 125.95812988281, 33159.5390625)
                                SP = 'Default'

                                local v195 = Vector3.new(61163.8515625, 5.342342376708984, 1819.7841796875)

                                shouldRequestEntrance(v195, 18000)
                            end
                        else
                            MMon = {
                                'Magma Ninja',
                                'Lava Pirate',
                            }
                            MPos = CFrame.new(-5428, 78, -5959)
                            SP = 'Default'
                        end
                    else
                        MMon = {
                            'Marine Captain',
                        }
                        MPos = CFrame.new(-2010.5059814453125, 73.00115966796875, -3326.620849609375)
                        SP = 'Default'
                    end
                elseif World3 then
                    if SelectMaterial ~= 'Scrap Metal' then
                        if SelectMaterial ~= 'Fish Tail' then
                            if SelectMaterial ~= 'Conjured Cocoa' then
                                if SelectMaterial ~= 'Dragon Scale' then
                                    if SelectMaterial ~= 'Gunpowder' then
                                        if SelectMaterial ~= 'Mini Tusk' then
                                            if SelectMaterial == 'Demonic Wisp' then
                                                MMon = {
                                                    'Demonic Soul',
                                                }
                                                MPos = CFrame.new(-9495.6806640625, 453.58624267578125, 5977.3486328125)
                                                SP = 'Default'
                                            end
                                        else
                                            MMon = {
                                                'Mythological Pirate',
                                            }
                                            MPos = CFrame.new(-13545, 470, -6917)
                                            SP = 'Default'
                                        end
                                    else
                                        MMon = {
                                            'Pistol Billionaire',
                                        }
                                        MPos = CFrame.new(-84.8556900024414, 85.62061309814453, 6132.0087890625)
                                        SP = 'Default'
                                    end
                                else
                                    MMon = {
                                        'Dragon Crew Archer',
                                        'Dragon Crew Warrior',
                                    }
                                    MPos = CFrame.new(6594, 383, 139)
                                    SP = 'Default'
                                end
                            else
                                MMon = {

                                        break

                                        if string.find(_Text, QuestNeta()[5]) then
                                            wait()

                                            if _G.Level and (v203.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (v203.Parent and plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible ~= false)) then
                                            end
                                        else
                                            replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AbandonQuest')
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                _tp(QuestNeta()[4])

                                if replicated:FindFirstChild(QuestNeta()[1]) then
                                    _tp(replicated:FindFirstChild(QuestNeta()[1]).HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

        function AuraSkin(p427)
            local v428 = {
                {
                    StorageName = p427,
                    Type = 'AuraSkin',
                    Context = 'Equip',

        function VaildColor(p429)
            if p429 and p429.BrickColor then
                return tostring(p429.BrickColor) == 'Lime green'
            end
        end
        function HakiCalculate(p430)
            local v431 = {
                ['Really red'] = 'Pure Red',
                Oyster = 'Snow White',
                ['Hot pink'] = 'Winter Sky',
            }

            if p430 and p430.BrickColor then
                return v431[tostring(p430.BrickColor)]
            end
        end

        function getT(p693)
            local v694 = nil

            if p693 == 1 then
                v694 = workspace.Map['Haunted Castle'].Tablet.Segment1.Line.Rotation
            elseif p693 == 3 then
                v694 = workspace.Map['Haunted Castle'].Tablet.Segment3.Line.Rotation
            elseif p693 == 4 then
                v694 = workspace.Map['Haunted Castle'].Tablet.Segment4.Line.Rotation
            elseif p693 == 7 then
                v694 = workspace.Map['Haunted Castle'].Tablet.Segment7.Line.Rotation
            elseif p693 == 10 then
                v694 = workspace.Map['Haunted Castle'].Tablet.Segment10.Line.Rotation
            end
            if v694 then
                return v694.Z
            end
        end
        function getRT(p695)
            local _Quest4 = workspace.Map['Haunted Castle'].Trophies.Quest
            local v697, v698, v699 = pairs(_Quest4:GetChildren())
            local v700 = nil

            while true do
                local v701

                v699, v701 = v697(v698, v699)

                if v699 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if p695 == 1 and (v701.Name == 'Trophy1' and v701:FindFirstChild('Handle')) then
                    v700 = v701.Handle.Rotation
                elseif p695 == 2 and (v701.Name == 'Trophy2' and v701:FindFirstChild('Handle')) then
                    v700 = v701.Handle.Rotation
                elseif p695 == 3 and (v701.Name == 'Trophy3' and v701:FindFirstChild('Handle')) then
                    v700 = v701.Handle.Rotation
                elseif p695 == 4 and (v701.Name == 'Trophy4' and v701:FindFirstChild('Handle')) then
                    v700 = v701.Handle.Rotation
                elseif p695 == 5 and (v701.Name == 'Trophy5' and v701:FindFirstChild('Handle')) then
                    v700 = v701.Handle.Rotation
                end
                if v700 then
                    return v700.Z
                end
            end
        end
        function GetFirePlacard(p702, p703)
            if tostring(workspace.Map['Haunted Castle']['Placard' .. p702][p703].Indicator.BrickColor) ~= 'Pearl' then
                fireclickdetector(workspace.Map['Haunted Castle']['Placard' .. p702][p703].ClickDetector)
            end
        end

        function GetSeaBeastTrial()
            if not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('FishmanTrial') then
                return nil
            end
            if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Trial of Water') then
                FishmanTrial = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Trial of Water')
            end
            if FishmanTrial then
                local v731 = next
                local v732, v733 = workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()

                while true do
                    local v734

                    v733, v734 = v731(v732, v733)

                    if v733 == nil then
                        break
                    end
                    if v734:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and ((v734.HumanoidRootPart.Position - FishmanTrial.Position).Magnitude <= 1500 and v734.Health.Value > 0) then
                        return v734
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        spawn(function()
            while wait(Sec) do
                pcall(function()
                    if _G.Complete_Trials and tostring(plr.Data.Race.Value) == 'Mink' then
                        notween(workspace.Map.MinkTrial.Ceiling.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -20, 0))
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        spawn(function()
            while wait(Sec) do
                pcall(function()
                    if _G.Complete_Trials and (tostring(plr.Data.Race.Value) == 'Fishman' and GetSeaBeastTrial()) then
                        repeat
                            task.wait()
                            spawn(function()
                                _tp(CFrame.new(GetSeaBeastTrial().HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, game:GetService('Workspace').Map['WaterBase-Plane'].Position.Y + 300, GetSeaBeastTrial().HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
                            end)

                            MousePos = GetSeaBeastTrial().HumanoidRootPart.Position

                            Useskills('Melee', 'Z')
                            Useskills('Melee', 'X')
                            Useskills('Melee', 'C')
                            wait(0.1)
                            Useskills('Sword', 'Z')
                            Useskills('Sword', 'X')
                            wait(0.1)
                            Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'Z')
                            Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'X')
                            Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'C')
                            wait(0.1)
                            Useskills('Gun', 'Z')
                            Useskills('Gun', 'X')
                        until _G.Complete_Trials == false or not GetSeaBeastTrial()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

                                        end
                                    else
                                        _G.Prehis_Find = true
                                        _G.Prehis_Skills = false
                                    end
                                end
                                if not (_G.Dojoo and v743) then
                                    _G.Prehis_Find = false
                                    _G.Prehis_Skills = false

                                    if not v743 then
                                        debug = false

                                        replicated.Modules.Net:FindFirstChild('RF/InteractDragonQuest'):InvokeServer(unpack({
                                            {
                                                NPC = 'Dojo Trainer',
                                                Command = 'ClaimQuest',
                                            },
                                        }))
                                    end

                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            if v744 == 'Orange' or v744 == 'Blue' then
                                return nil
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

        function checkQuesta()
            local u747 = {
                {
                    Context = 'Check',
                },
            }
            local u748 = nil

            pcall(function()
                local v749 = {
                    {
                        Context = 'RequestQuest',
                    },
                }

                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):WaitForChild('Modules'):WaitForChild('Net'):WaitForChild('RF/DragonHunter'):InvokeServer(unpack(v749))
            end)

            local _, _ = pcall(function()
                u748 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):WaitForChild('Modules'):WaitForChild('Net'):WaitForChild('RF/DragonHunter'):InvokeServer(unpack(u747))
            end)
            local v750 = nil
            local v751 = nil
            local v752 = nil
            local v753, v754

            if u748 and u748.Text then
                v753 = true

                local _Text2 = u748.Text

                if string.find(tostring(_Text2), 'Defeat') then
                    local v756 = string.sub(tostring(_Text2), 8, 9)

                    v750 = tonumber(v756)

                    local v757, v758, v759 = pairs({
                        'Hydra Enforcer',
                        'Venomous Assailant',
                    })

                    v752 = 1

                    while true do
                        v759, v754 = v757(v758, v759)

                        if v759 == nil then
                            v754 = v751

                            break
                        end
                        if string.find(_Text2, v754) then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                elseif string.find(tostring(_Text2), 'Destroy') then
                    v754 = nil
                    v750 = 10
                    v752 = 2
                else
                    v754 = v751
                end
            else
                v754 = v751
                v753 = false
            end

            return v753, v754, v750, v752
        end
        function BackTODoJo()
            local v760, v761, v762 = pairs(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Notifications:GetChildren())

            while true do
                local v763

                v762, v763 = v760(v761, v762)

                if v762 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v763.Name == 'NotificationTemplate' and string.find(v763.Text, 'Head back to the Dojo to complete more tasks') then
                    return true
                end
            end

            return false
        end
        function DragonMobClear(p764, p765, p766)
            if workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild(p765) then
                local v767, v768, v769 = pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren())

                while true do
                    local v770

                    v769, v770 = v767(v768, v769)

                    if v769 == nil then
                        break
                    end
                    if v770.Name == p765 and (u19.Alive(v770) and p764) then
                        u19.Kill(v770, p764)
                    end
                end
            else
                _tp(p766)
            end
        end

                            local v816

                            if v815 == nil then

                                v816.Parent:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

            while wait() do

                        return

        function isnil(p1009)
            return p1009 == nil
        end

        local function u1011(p1010)
            return math.floor(tonumber(p1010) + 0.5)
        end

        Number = math.random(1, 1000000)

        function EspPly()
            local v1012 = next
            local v1013, v1014 = game.Players:GetChildren()

            while true do
                local u1015

                v1014, u1015 = v1012(v1013, v1014)

                if v1014 == nil then
                    break
                end

                pcall(function()
                    if not isnil(u1015.Character) then
                        if PlayerEsp then
                            if isnil(u1015.Character.Head) or u1015.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
                                u1015.Character.Head['NameEsp' .. Number].TextLabel.Text = u1015.Data.Level.Value .. ' | ' .. u1015.Name .. ' | ' .. u1011((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - u1015.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' M\nHealth : ' .. u1011(u1015.Character.Humanoid.Health * 100 / u1015.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth) .. '%'
                            else
                                local _BillboardGui = Instance.new('BillboardGui', u1015.Character.Head)

                                _BillboardGui.Name = 'NameEsp' .. Number
                                _BillboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                                _BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
                                _BillboardGui.Adornee = u1015.Character.Head
                                _BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true

                                local _TextLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel', _BillboardGui)

                                _TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                                _TextLabel.FontSize = 'Size14'
                                _TextLabel.TextWrapped = true
                                _TextLabel.Text = u1015.Name .. ' \n' .. u1011((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - u1015.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' M'
                                _TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                                _TextLabel.TextYAlignment = 'Top'
                                _TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                                _TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5

                                if u1015.Team ~= TeamSelf then
                                    _TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(255, 0, 0)
                                else
                                    _TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 254)
                                end
                            end
                        elseif u1015.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
                            u1015.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number):Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        function ChestEsp()
            if ChestESP then
                local _CollectionService4 = game:GetService('CollectionService')
                local _LocalPlayer8 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
                local _Position5 = (_LocalPlayer8.Character or _LocalPlayer8.CharacterAdded:Wait()):GetPivot().Position
                local __ChestTagged3 = _CollectionService4:GetTagged('_ChestTagged')
                local v1070, v1071, v1072 = ipairs(__ChestTagged3)

                while true do
                    local u1073

                    v1072, u1073 = v1070(v1071, v1072)

                    if v1072 == nil then
                        break
                    end

                    local v1074

                    if SelectedIsland and not u1073:IsDescendantOf(SelectedIsland) or u1073:GetAttribute('IsDisabled') then
                        v1074 = true
                    end

                    local v1075, v1076 = pcall(function()
                        return u1073:GetPivot().Position
                    end)

                    if not v1075 then
                        if not u1073:IsA('BasePart') then
                            v1074 = true
                        end

                        v1076 = u1073.Position
                    end

                    local _Magnitude5 = (v1076 - _Position5).Magnitude

                    u1073:GetFullName():gsub('[^%w_]', '_')

                    local _ChestEspAttachment = u1073:FindFirstChild('ChestEspAttachment')

                    if not _ChestEspAttachment then
                        local _Attachment = Instance.new('Attachment')

                        _Attachment.Name = 'ChestEspAttachment'
                        _Attachment.Parent = u1073
                        _Attachment.Position = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

                        local _BillboardGui10 = Instance.new('BillboardGui')

                        _BillboardGui10.Name = 'NameEsp'
                        _BillboardGui10.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 30)
                        _BillboardGui10.Adornee = _Attachment
                        _BillboardGui10.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                        _BillboardGui10.AlwaysOnTop = true
                        _BillboardGui10.Parent = _Attachment

                        local _TextLabel10 = Instance.new('TextLabel')

                        _TextLabel10.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                        _TextLabel10.TextSize = 14
                        _TextLabel10.TextWrapped = true
                        _TextLabel10.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                        _TextLabel10.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
                        _TextLabel10.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        _TextLabel10.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                        _TextLabel10.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 245, 245)
                        _TextLabel10.Parent = _BillboardGui10
                    end

                    local v1082

                    if _ChestEspAttachment then
                        v1082 = _ChestEspAttachment:FindFirstChild('NameEsp')
                    else
                        v1082 = _ChestEspAttachment
                    end
                    if v1082 then
                        local v1083 = math.floor(_Magnitude5 / 3)
                        local _Label = u1073.Name:gsub('Label', '')

                        v1082.TextLabel.Text = string.format('[%s] %d M', _Label, v1083)
                    end
                    if _G_AutoFarmChest and (_Magnitude5 <= 20 and _ChestEspAttachment) then
                        _ChestEspAttachment:Destroy()

                        v1074 = true
                    else
                        v1074 = true
                    end
                    if not v1074 then
                        break
                    end
                end
            else
                local v1085, v1086, v1087 = ipairs(game:GetService('CollectionService'):GetTagged('_ChestTagged'))

                while true do
                    local v1088

                    v1087, v1088 = v1085(v1086, v1087)

                    if v1087 == nil then
                        break
                    end

                    local _ChestEspAttachment2 = v1088:FindFirstChild('ChestEspAttachment')

                    if _ChestEspAttachment2 then
                        _ChestEspAttachment2:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        function berriesEsp()
            if BerryEsp then
                local _CollectionService5 = game:GetService('CollectionService')
                local _LocalPlayer9 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
                local _BerryBush2 = _CollectionService5:GetTagged('BerryBush')
                local v1093, v1094, v1095 = ipairs(_BerryBush2)

                while true do
                    local v1096

                    v1095, v1096 = v1093(v1094, v1095)

                    if v1095 == nil then
                        break
                    end

                    local _Position6 = v1096.Parent:GetPivot().Position
                    local v1098, v1099, v1100 = pairs(v1096:GetAttributes())

                    while true do
                        local v1101

                        v1100, v1101 = v1098(v1099, v1100)

                        if v1100 == nil then
                            break
                        end
                        if v1101 and (not BerryArray or table.find(BerryArray, v1101)) then
                            local v1102 = 'BerryEspPart_' .. v1101 .. '_' .. tostring(_Position6)
                            local v1103 = workspace:FindFirstChild(v1102)

                            if not v1103 then
                                v1103 = Instance.new('Part')
                                v1103.Name = v1102
                                v1103.Transparency = 1
                                v1103.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                                v1103.Anchored = true
                                v1103.CanCollide = false
                                v1103.Parent = workspace
                                v1103.CFrame = CFrame.new(_Position6)
                            end
                            if not v1103:FindFirstChild('NameEsp') then
                                local _BillboardGui11 = Instance.new('BillboardGui', v1103)

                                _BillboardGui11.Name = 'NameEsp'
                                _BillboardGui11.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                                _BillboardGui11.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 30)
                                _BillboardGui11.Adornee = v1103
                                _BillboardGui11.AlwaysOnTop = true

                                local _TextLabel11 = Instance.new('TextLabel', _BillboardGui11)

                                _TextLabel11.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                                _TextLabel11.TextSize = 14
                                _TextLabel11.TextWrapped = true
                                _TextLabel11.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                                _TextLabel11.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
                                _TextLabel11.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                                _TextLabel11.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                                _TextLabel11.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 245, 245)
                                _TextLabel11.Parent = _BillboardGui11
                            end

                            local _NameEsp = v1103:FindFirstChild('NameEsp')
                            local v1107 = (_LocalPlayer9.Character.Head.Position - _Position6).Magnitude / 3

                            _NameEsp.TextLabel.Text = '[' .. v1101 .. ']' .. ' ' .. math.round(v1107) .. ' M'

                            if _G.AutoBerry and math.round(v1107) <= 20 then
                                v1103:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                local v1108, v1109, v1110 = ipairs(workspace:GetChildren())

                while true do
                    local v1111

                    v1110, v1111 = v1108(v1109, v1110)

                    if v1110 == nil then
                        break
                    end
                    if v1111:IsA('Part') and v1111.Name:match('BerryEspPart_.*') then
                        v1111:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local _LocalPlayer10 = game.Players.LocalPlayer

        local function u1241(p1239)
            if not p1239 then
                return false
            end

            local _Humanoid3 = p1239:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')

            if _Humanoid3 then
                _Humanoid3 = _Humanoid3.Health > 0
            end

            return _Humanoid3
        end
        local function u1258(p1242, p1243)
            local v1244 = game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren()
            local v1245 = game:GetService('Players'):GetPlayers()
            local _Position7 = p1242:GetPivot().Position
            local v1247, v1248, v1249 = ipairs(v1244)
            local v1250 = {}

            while true do
                local v1251

                v1249, v1251 = v1247(v1248, v1249)

                if v1249 == nil then
                    break
                end

                local _HumanoidRootPart3 = v1251:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')

                if _HumanoidRootPart3 and (u1241(v1251) and (_HumanoidRootPart3.Position - _Position7).Magnitude <= p1243) then
                    table.insert(v1250, v1251)
                end
            end

            local v1253, v1254, v1255 = ipairs(v1245)

            while true do
                local v1256

                v1255, v1256 = v1253(v1254, v1255)

                if v1255 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v1256 ~= _LocalPlayer10 and v1256.Character then
                    local _HumanoidRootPart4 = v1256.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')

                    if _HumanoidRootPart4 and (u1241(v1256.Character) and (_HumanoidRootPart4.Position - _Position7).Magnitude <= p1243) then
                        table.insert(v1250, v1256.Character)
                    end
                end
            end

            return v1250
        end

        function AttackNoCoolDown()
            local _LocalPlayer11 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
            local _Character9 = _LocalPlayer11.Character

            if not _Character9 then
                return
            end

            local v1261, v1262, v1263 = ipairs(_Character9:GetChildren())
            local v1264 = nil

            while true do
                local v1265

                v1263, v1265 = v1261(v1262, v1263)

                if v1263 == nil then
                    v1265 = v1264

                    break
                end
                if v1265:IsA('Tool') then
                    break
                end
            end

            if v1265 then
                local v1266 = u1258(_Character9, 60)

                if #v1266 ~= 0 then

        function get_Monster()
            local v1286, v1287, v1288 = pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren())

            while true do
                local v1289

                v1288, v1289 = v1286(v1287, v1288)

                if v1288 == nil then
                    break

                local v1290 = v1289:FindFirstChild('UpperTorso') or v1289:FindFirstChild('Head')

                if v1289:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart', true) and (v1290 and (v1289.Head.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 50) then
                    return true, v1290.Position
                end
            end

            local v1291, v1292, v1293 = pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren())

            while true do
                local v1294

                v1293, v1294 = v1291(v1292, v1293)

                if v1293 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v1294:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (v1294:FindFirstChild('Health') and v1294.Health.Value > 0) then
                    return true, v1294.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                end
            end

            local v1295, v1296, v1297 = pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren())

            while true do
                local v1298

                v1297, v1298 = v1295(v1296, v1297)

                if v1297 == nil then

                if v1298:FindFirstChild('Health') and (v1298.Health.Value > 0 and v1298:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat')) then
                    return true, v1298.Engine.Position
                end
            end
        end

                end
            end
        end
